45:  def _report_state(self,key):
46:   """Return 'complete', 'incomplete' or 'missing' for a stage report."""
47:   path=self.stage_report_path(key)
48:   if not path or not path.is_file():return 'missing'
49:   import json
50:   try:payload=json.loads(path.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
51:   except (OSError,ValueError):return 'missing'
52:   if not isinstance(payload,dict):return 'missing'
53:   # A cancelled sweep still writes its report; without this flag the pipeline
54:   # advanced on partial data. Applies to every stage now: the PDF writer marks
55:   # its report partial on cancel too. Reports written before 0.4C.7 have no flag
56:   # and keep the old behaviour so upgrades do not re-run everything.
57:   if payload.get('complete') is False:return 'incomplete'
58:   return 'complete'

210:  def _quarantine_report(self,report_path):
211:   """Rename a stale handoff report so the pipeline cannot advance onto it.
212: 
213:   When a gate rejects every item the stage did not run, but its previous report
214:   is still on disk and still looks current. Leaving it there meant the next
215:   stage ran against stale data and the failing stage never reached a terminal
216:   state, so every resume re-raised the same error.
217:   """
218:   try:
219:    stale=Path(str(report_path)+'.stale');report_path.replace(stale);return stale
220:   except OSError:
221:    report_path.unlink(missing_ok=True);return None
222:  def _gate_blocked(self,stage,report_path,error,rejected=None):
223:   """Report a gate that accepted nothing, quarantine the stale report, return False."""
224:   message=str(error);rejected=list(rejected or [])
225:   _,_,detail=message.partition(':')
226:   detail=detail.strip()
227:   # Detail is "IDENTITY=ERR1,ERR2; IDENTITY2=...". A leading "IDENTITY=..." form
228:   # is used by the writer, whose code prefix is followed by a space.
229:   # Entries without '=' carry no errors but are still reported.
230:   if detail and not rejected:
231:    for chunk in detail.split(';'):
232:     chunk=chunk.strip()
233:     if not chunk:continue
234:     identity,_,errors=chunk.partition('=')
235:     rejected.append({'identity':identity.strip() or '?','errors':[e.strip() for e in errors.split(',') if e.strip()]})
236:   if rejected:self._report_rejected(stage,rejected)
237:   self._quarantine_report(report_path) if report_path else None
238:   self.refresh_pipeline()
239:   if not rejected:self.status.setText(f'{stage}: nothing to process - {message}');return False
240:   summary=f'{len(rejected)} drawing(s) rejected, 0 accepted.'
241:   self.status.setText(f'{stage}: {summary} Nothing was written.')
242:   # `self` is a QMainWindow, so QMessageBox.warning is the QWidget overload
243:   # (parent, title, text) - unlike the static convenience call.
244:   box=QMessageBox(QMessageBox.Warning,f'{stage} - nothing to process',f'{summary}\n\n'+'\n'.join(f'{item.get("identity","?")}: {", ".join(item.get("errors",[]))}' for item in rejected[:10])+'\n\nThe previous handoff report was moved aside so the pipeline cannot advance on stale data. Fix the listed drawings, then run this stage again.',QMessageBox.Ok,self)
245:   box.setObjectName('gateBlockedDialog');box.show();box.raise_()
246:   return False

from dataclasses import dataclass,asdict,fields
from pathlib import Path
import json
@dataclass
class Settings:
    source_folder:str=''; destination_root:str=''; destination_bundle_name:str='Nederwiek_Bundle'; core_console:str=''
    project_from:str='IJMUIDEN VER GAMMA'; project_to:str='NEDERWIEK BETA'; drawing_from:str='2013J310015'; drawing_to:str='I301-J30019'
    long_from:str='OEPA230146'; long_to:str='OEPA240101'; short_from:str='230146'; short_to:str='240101'; target_layer:str='NEDERWIEK_DATA'
    contractor_doc_from:str='2GWG'; contractor_doc_to:str='2GWN'
    workers:int=4
    def __post_init__(self):
        try:self.workers=min(16,max(1,int(self.workers)))
        except (TypeError,ValueError):self.workers=4
    @classmethod
    def load(cls,p):
        p=Path(p)
        if not p.exists():return cls()
        try:data=json.loads(p.read_text(encoding='utf-8'))
        except (OSError,ValueError):return cls()
        if not isinstance(data,dict):return cls()
        known={field.name for field in fields(cls)}
        return cls(**{key:value for key,value in data.items() if key in known})
    def save(self,p):
        p=Path(p);p.parent.mkdir(parents=True,exist_ok=True);p.write_text(json.dumps(asdict(self),indent=2),encoding='utf-8')

21: class _FakeBox:
22:     """Stand-in for ``QMessageBox(...)`` used in widget style.
23: 
24:     ``_gate_blocked`` builds a box explicitly (``QMessageBox(...)`` +
25:     ``show()``) instead of calling the static convenience method, so patching
26:     only ``QMessageBox.information`` is not enough to keep a test headless.
27:     """
28: 
29:     Warning = error = information = Question = 0
30:     Ok = 1
31: 
32:     def __init__(self, *args, **kwargs):
33:         self.args = args
34:

36:         return None
37:

39:         return None
40: 
41:     def raise_(self):
42:         return None
43:

45:         return 1

48: def _silence_message_boxes():
49:     """Replace both dialog styles; returns state for _restore_message_boxes."""
50:     saved = {name: getattr(legacy.QMessageBox, name)
51:              for name in ("information", "warning", "critical", "exec", "question")}
52:     legacy.QMessageBox.information = staticmethod(lambda *a, **k: legacy.QMessageBox.Ok)
53:     legacy.QMessageBox.warning = staticmethod(lambda *a, **k: legacy.QMessageBox.Ok)
54:     legacy.QMessageBox.critical = staticmethod(lambda *a, **k: legacy.QMessageBox.Ok)
55:     legacy.QMessageBox.exec = lambda self, *a, **k: 1
56:     return saved
57: 
58: 
59: def _restore_message_boxes(saved):
60:     for name, value in saved.items():
61:         setattr(legacy.QMessageBox, name, value)

64: class _DialogPatchMixin:

72:         # dialogs stay silenced until the widget and temp dir are gone.
73:         _restore_message_boxes(self._saved_dialogs)

147:         # Patch both dialog styles: the 3-arg ``QMessageBox.information(...)``
148:         # convenience call and the widget-style ``QMessageBox(...)`` chain. An
149:         # unpatched modal here would block the whole suite forever.
150:         shown = _silence_message_boxes()
151:         try:
152:             self.main.run_next_stage()
153:         finally:
154:             _restore_message_boxes(shown)
155:         self.assertEqual(started, [])
156:         self.assertEqual(
157:             self.main.centralWidget().tabText(self.main.centralWidget().currentIndex()),
158:             "Reports",
159:         )

174: def _mean_rgb(image):
175:     """Average colour of the visible pixels, or None when fully transparent."""
176:     total = count = 0
177:     for y in range(image.height()):
178:         for x in range(image.width()):
179:             color = image.pixelColor(x, y)
180:             if color.alpha() > 40:
181:                 total += color.red() + color.green() + color.blue()
182:                 count += 1
183:     return (total / count) if count else None
184:

541:     def show_reports_tab(self) -> None:
542:         """Bring the Reports page forward in the sidebar shell.
543: 
544:         app.Main assumes its central widget is still the legacy QTabWidget. The
545:         modern shell swaps that for a QStackedWidget, so the inherited method
546:         raised ``AttributeError: 'QWidget' object has no attribute 'count'`` as
547:         soon as "Open Reports" (or the scan row) called it. Navigation is pure
548:         presentation, so it belongs here rather than in the controller.
549:         """
550:         try:
551:             index = self._page_names.index("Reports")
552:         except (AttributeError, ValueError):
553:             return
554:         self._show_page(index)
555:         if 0 <= index < len(self.nav_buttons):
556:             self.nav_buttons[index].setChecked(True)
557: 
558:     def _apply_widget_roles(self) -> None:
559:         if hasattr(self, "primary"):
560:             self.primary.setObjectName("primaryButton")
561:         # The QSS targets QSpinBox#workerInput / QLabel#workerLabel; without these
562:         # object names the worker controls fell back to the default Fusion look.
563:         if hasattr(self, "workers"):
564:             self.workers.setObjectName("workerInput")
565:         if hasattr(self, "adv"):
566:             self.adv.setObjectName("advancedGroup")
567:         for line_edit in self.findChildren(legacy.QLineEdit):
568:             line_edit.setMinimumHeight(38)
569:             line_edit.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
570:         for form in self.findChildren(legacy.QFormLayout):
571:             form.setVerticalSpacing(10)
572:             form.setHorizontalSpacing(14)
573:             form.setFieldGrowthPolicy(legacy.QFormLayout.AllNonFixedFieldsGrow)
574:         if hasattr(self, "adv"):
575:             self.adv.setVisible(True)
576:             self.adv.setMinimumHeight(82)
577:         for widget in self.findChildren(QLabel):
578:             if widget.text().strip() == "Parallel workers":
579:                 widget.setObjectName("workerLabel")
580:         for widget in self.findChildren(QPushButton):
581:             text = widget.text().strip().lower()
582:             if any(token in text for token in ("cancel", "stop")):
583:                 widget.setObjectName("dangerButton")
584:             widget.setCursor(Qt.PointingHandCursor)
585:         for widget in self.findChildren(QAbstractButton):
586:             widget.setFocusPolicy(Qt.StrongFocus)
587: 
588:         for table in self.findChildren(legacy.QTableWidget):
589:             table.setAlternatingRowColors(True)
590:             table.setShowGrid(False)
591:             table.setSelectionBehavior(legacy.QAbstractItemView.SelectRows)
592:             table.verticalHeader().setVisible(False)
593: 
594:     def _repolish_button_palette(self) -> None:
595:         """Force Qt to re-evaluate object-name based QSS selectors."""
596:         widgets = [self]
597:         widgets.extend(self.findChildren(QPushButton))
598:         if hasattr(self, "adv"):
599:             widgets.append(self.adv)
600:         for widget in widgets:
601:             style = widget.style()
602:             style.unpolish(widget)
603:             style.polish(widget)
604:             widget.update()
605: 
606:     def _refresh_modern_status(self) -> None:
607:         busy = bool(getattr(self, "_busy", False))
608:         stage = getattr(self, "_running_stage", None)
609:         if busy:
610:             label = str(stage or "WORKING").replace("_", " ").upper()
611:         elif getattr(self, "_chain", False):
612:             label = "PIPELINE ACTIVE"
613:             busy = True
614:         else:
615:             label = "READY"
616:         self.modern_status_badge.setText(label)
617:         self.modern_status_badge.setProperty("busy", busy)
618:         self.modern_status_badge.style().unpolish(self.modern_status_badge)
619:         self.modern_status_badge.style().polish(self.modern_status_badge)
620: 
621:     # Publish-gate loop protection lives in app.Main. ModernMain deliberately does
622:     # not re-define next_stage/_stage_complete/refresh_pipeline/_advance_pipeline:
623:     # the previous copies drifted from the originals (skipped-stage handling and
624:     # refresh_primary were both lost), which is exactly the bug this shell exists
625:     # to avoid. tests/test_app_shell_parity.py enforces the rule.
626: 
627:

127: def prune_job_root(root, keep_days=7, now=None):
128:     """Remove job folders under *root* older than ``keep_days``.
129: 
130:     ``trusted_runtime`` is never touched: the LISP copies there are rewritten
131:     on every engine start, but deleting them mid-run would break a live worker.
132:     Returns ``(removed, failed)`` counts; never raises.
133:     """
134:     root = Path(root)
135:     if not root.is_dir():
136:         return 0, 0
137:     cutoff = (now or time.time()) - keep_days * 86400
138:     removed = failed = 0
139:     for child in root.iterdir():
140:         if not child.is_dir():
141:             continue
142:         if child.name == "trusted_runtime":
143:             continue
144:         try:
145:             if child.stat().st_mtime > cutoff:
146:                 continue
147:             shutil.rmtree(child, ignore_errors=False)
148:             removed += 1
149:         except OSError:
150:             failed += 1
151:     return removed, failed

65:     `cancel_shutdown()` the global registry stayed latched and any later
66:     `run_console` was killed on spawn (returncode -15, empty output). The suite
67:     only exposed this once a GUI test closed a window before this test ran.
68:     """

76:         for name in ("information", "warning", "critical"):
77:             setattr(QMessageBox, name, staticmethod(lambda *a, **k: QMessageBox.Ok))
78:         QMessageBox.exec = lambda self, *a, **k: 1
79:         try:

82:             import runtime.process_tracking as tracking
83: 
84:             main = legacy.Main()

89:                 capture_output=True,
90:             )

92:             self.assertIn(b"ok", result.stdout)
93:             main.deleteLater()
94:         finally:
95:             for name, value in saved.items():
96:                 setattr(QMessageBox, name, value)

99: class PruneJobRootTests(unittest.TestCase):
100:     def setUp(self):
101:         self.tmp = tempfile.TemporaryDirectory()
102:         self.root = Path(self.tmp.name) / "GNI"
103:         self.root.mkdir()
104: 
105:     def tearDown(self):
106:         self.tmp.cleanup()
107: 
108:     def _job(self, name, age_days):
109:         folder = self.root / name
110:         folder.mkdir()
111:         (folder / "file.txt").write_text("x", encoding="utf-8")
112:         stamp = time.time() - age_days * 86400
113:         os.utime(folder, (stamp, stamp))
114:         return folder
115: 
116:     def test_old_jobs_are_removed_and_recent_jobs_kept(self):
117:         old = self._job("old_job", 30)
118:         recent = self._job("recent_job", 1)
119:         removed, failed = prune_job_root(self.root, keep_days=7)
120:         self.assertEqual((removed, failed), (1, 0))
121:         self.assertFalse(old.exists())
122:         self.assertTrue(recent.exists())
123: 
124:     def test_trusted_runtime_is_never_removed(self):
125:         trusted = self._job("trusted_runtime", 365)
126:         removed, _ = prune_job_root(self.root, keep_days=7)
127:         self.assertEqual(removed, 0)
128:         self.assertTrue(trusted.exists())
129: 
130:     def test_missing_root_is_not_an_error(self):
131:         self.assertEqual(prune_job_root(self.root / "nope", keep_days=7), (0, 0))

## Assistant

Analysis is done. Here's what I found, and the plan — **please confirm before I write code.**

## What exists today

`app.py::Main` (legacy controller) builds the Setup page:

```
QLineEdit src / dst / bundle / exe   (browse rows)
QLineEdit ×10 mapping (project/drawing/long/short/contractor from→to)
Migration pipeline table + self.primary  → "Run: …" / "Publish N validated pair(s)" / "Open Reports"
Cancel Safely | Parallel workers
Advanced group (per-stage re-run buttons)
```

State that drives "job" is spread across: `self.files, groups, support, support_results, discovery_results, revision_plans, _skipped, _chain, _running_stage, _busy, prog`, plus five tables (`tbl, stbl, dtbl, ptbl, rtbl`), and everything is derived from **report files on disk** (`_stage_complete` / `next_stage` read `<dst>/<bundle>/_GNI_REPORTS/*.json`). There is currently no "new job" path at all — only closing the app.

## The design I propose

**1. One method on `app.py::Main` (single source of truth), e.g. `finance`-style one-liners matching the file:**
- `job_finished()` → `True` when `self.files and not self._busy and not self._chain and self.next_stage() is None` (i.e. PDF stage complete, not just "Open Reports").
- `new_job()` — guarded, asks `QMessageBox.question` "Start a new job?", then clears:
  - `files, groups, support, support_results, discovery_results, revision_plans = []`, `_skipped=set()`, `_chain=False`, `_running_stage=None`
  - empties `tbl / stbl / dtbl / ptbl` (row count 0)
  - `prog` → 0, status line → "Ready…", `refresh_pipeline()`
  - `_started_destination` / `_job_bundle` memory reset (see point 4)
- `refresh_pipeline()` grows one line: `self.newjobb.setEnabled(not self._busy and self.job_finished())`, so the button enables itself the moment the PDF stage lands and disables again once anything starts.

**2. Placement:** a new `self.newjobb = QPushButton('New Job (clear inputs)')` added to the pipeline group's VBox **directly after `self.primary`** (before the *Cancel | Parallel workers* row), wired to `new_job()`. In `app_modern.py::_apply_widget_roles()` it gets `setObjectName('secondaryButton')` + a neutral QSS block (white/outline, not green like primary). No pipeline logic goes into `app_modern.py` — `tests/test_app_shell_parity.py` enforces that, and I'll add `new_job`/`job_finished` to its forbidden-override list.

**3. What gets cleared vs. kept**
- **Cleared:** every in-memory job result, all result tables, skipped-stage set, chain flag, progress, status.
- **Kept:** the four path fields + the ten mapping fields (they are `Settings` persisted into `config/settings.json`, a machine-local default — retyping "IJMUIDEN VER GAMMA" every run would be a regression). The mapping *values* are part of the *next* job's plan.
- **Next job starts clean** because `next_stage()` returns `('scan','Scan source')` as soon as `self.files` is empty — no report files need deleting.

**4. ⚠️ The one real decision — reuse of Destination root + Bundle name (I need your call here)**

If the operator keeps the same `Destination root` + `Bundle name`, the old `_GNI_REPORTS/*.json` files are still on disk. The pipeline itself won't advance early (scan is empty → stages show PENDING), **but** old checkpoints make the resume logic skip work: e.g. `stable_writer_checkpoint.json` / `pdf_publish_report.json` will mark pairs `PDF_UNCHANGED` and the writer "skipped" if source hashes match. So "new job, same bundle" would silently produce a half-empty run.

Options:
- **(A) Recommended – remember the job's bundle once `destination_manifest.json` is written, and block `New Job` with a clear dialog while `Destination root`/`Bundle name` still point at that same existing bundle.** Message: *"Change Destination root or Bundle name first (or move the old bundle aside), then press New Job."* Nothing on disk is ever deleted, matching the repo's read-only/backup rules.
- **(B) Archive the old bundle's `_GNI_REPORTS` to `_GNI_REPORTS_archive_<timestamp>` automatically** on New Job, then clear. Convenient, but it mutates the destination tree without asking.
- **(C) Just clear UI state** and leave the resume/checkpoint trap in place (not recommended — it can look like a successful run that did nothing).

**5. Cancel-safety:** `job_finished()` requires `not self._busy and not self._chain`, so the button can never be pressed mid-run, and `new_job()` re-checks and returns early if a worker/`bg.worker` is still running — same guard style as `closeEvent`.

**6. Tests** (stdlib unittest, in `tests/test_pipeline_navigation.py`, reusing the existing `_DialogPatchMixin`):
- disabled at start / during a `_write_all()` incomplete pipeline
- enabled exactly when `next_stage() is None` and idle
- `new_job()` clears `files/support/results/_skipped` and empties the tables, and the primary goes back to "Run migration to publish gate: Scan source"
- `new_job()` is a no-op while `_busy`
- `app_modern` does not re-implement `new_job` / `job_finished`
- plus the option-A block test if you pick A.

**7. Bookkeeping:** per AGENTS.md I'll first copy `app.py`, `app_modern.py`, `tests/test_pipeline_navigation.py`, `tests/test_app_shell_parity.py` into `backup_before_0_4C_15_<timestamp>/`, and bump the window title to `(0.4C.15 - new job reset)`.

---

**Confirm (or change) these four points and I'll implement:**
1. Button label/wording: **"New Job (clear inputs)"** / "Start new job — clear all results"?
2. Destination root + bundle fields **kept** as-is (only results cleared) — OK?
3. Bundle-reuse handling: **A** (block + explain, recommended), **B** (auto-archive reports), or **C** (UI only)?
4. New button placement right under `primary`, inside the *Migration pipeline* group — OK?

<skill_content name="OpenCode">
# Skill: OpenCode

# OpenCode

Use this guide as the starting point for work involving OpenCode itself. It
covers the core concepts needed to configure and customize OpenCode, extend it
with plugins, and build integrations with the OpenCode SDK, clients, and API.

Full documentation is available at <https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/>. This overview is
only an index of core concepts. Before answering a question about a topic below,
fetch the URL named in that section and use the full page as the source of
truth. Follow links from that page when the question needs more detail. Fetch
<https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/> first when you need to discover the relevant
documentation page.

A machine-readable documentation index is available at
<https://opencode.ai/v2/llms.txt>.

## Version policy

Always answer for OpenCode V2 unless the user explicitly asks about V1,
legacy OpenCode, or migrating from V1.

Use only <https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/> documentation as the source of truth for V2.
Do not use <https://opencode.ai/docs/>, which documents V1, and do not use
general web search to resolve a V2 documentation question when the V2 docs or
linked pages cover it. The schema served from
<https://opencode.ai/config.json> accepts both V2 fields and deprecated V1
fields. Never recommend a field it marks deprecated. If V2 documentation is
missing or contradictory, state the uncertainty or ask for clarification instead of falling back to V1.

V1 documentation and syntax may be consulted only when the user explicitly
asks about V1 or when needed as migration input. Outputs and recommendations
must still use V2 unless the user specifically requests a V1 result.

## [CLI](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli)

For questions about the terminal interface, command-line invocation, `run`,
`mini`, terminal providers, or other CLI behavior, fetch the
[CLI guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli) and the relevant page linked from
that section.

CLI and TUI preferences are separate from OpenCode's server and project
configuration. They live in the global `~/.config/opencode/cli.json`, or
`$XDG_CONFIG_HOME/opencode/cli.json` when `XDG_CONFIG_HOME` is set. There is no
project-local CLI configuration. Set `OPENCODE_CLI_CONFIG_CONTENT` to merge
inline JSON over the global settings. Most preferences can also be changed from
the TUI by pressing `Ctrl+P` and selecting **Open settings**.

### [Settings](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli/config)

Fetch the full [CLI settings reference](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli/config)
before editing `cli.json`. It documents every terminal-only setting, accepted
values, and examples, including themes, input, sessions, tabs, diffs, alerts,
Mini, keybindings, terminal plugins, and debugging. Do not put these settings
in `opencode.json(c)`.

### [Keybinds](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli/keybinds)

Configure keybindings under `keybinds` in `cli.json`. The leader key is the
`keybinds.leader` entry; leader timing is configured separately under
`leader.timeout`. Bindings can use a string, an array of strings, or an object
when event behavior such as `preventDefault` is required. Disable a binding
with `"none"` or `false`.

Never guess a command ID, default binding, or accepted key syntax. Fetch the
full [keybind reference](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli/keybinds), which lists
the current IDs and defaults, before answering or editing a binding.

## [OpenCode configuration](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/config)

OpenCode's server and project configuration uses JSON or JSONC. Include the
published schema so the user's editor can validate fields and provide
autocomplete:

```jsonc
{
  "$schema": "https://opencode.ai/config.json",
}
```

Global configuration lives at `~/.config/opencode/opencode.json(c)` and applies
to every project for that user. Project configuration can live in any directory
as `opencode.json(c)` or `.opencode/opencode.json(c)`, including nested packages
in a monorepo.

During ordinary project discovery, OpenCode searches the current Location
directory and every ancestor through the filesystem root, including directories
above the detected project or repository root. It merges direct
`opencode.json(c)` files from the farthest ancestor to the current directory,
then does the same for `.opencode/opencode.json(c)` files. This means every
discovered `.opencode` config overrides every discovered direct config. Global
filesystem configuration has lower precedence than these discovered documents.

Common configuration fields include `model`, `default_agent`, `permissions`,
`agents`, `commands`, `plugins`, `providers`, `mcp`, `skills`, `instructions`,
`references`, `formatter`, and `lsp`.

This configuration is distinct from `cli.json`. Use the
[CLI settings reference](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/cli/config) for terminal
preferences, especially themes and keybindings.

Config changes apply without a restart. OpenCode watches config files and
reloads agents, commands, skills, MCP servers, and plugins after a save. The TUI
does the same for `cli.json`. To force a full reload, use `/reload`. Don't tell
the user to restart OpenCode after editing config.

Do not guess field names or shapes. Fetch the V2 configuration guide and its
linked topic guide as the source of truth, and preserve unrelated settings when
editing an existing file. Keep the published `$schema` URL in configuration
examples, but do not fetch it to determine the V2 configuration shape.

See the [full configuration guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/config) for
every field, examples, config locations, and links to dedicated feature guides.

## [MCP servers](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/mcp-servers)

Configure MCP servers under `mcp.servers`. Prefer the CLI because it preserves
unrelated configuration. Use `--global` when the user asks to set up a service
for themselves without limiting it to the current project; omit it when they
explicitly want project-local configuration.

```sh
opencode mcp add <name> --global --url <remote-url>
opencode mcp list
```

Remote servers use OAuth by default. If `mcp list` reports that a server needs
authentication, tell the user to run `/mcps`, select the server, and sign in.
Do not run `opencode mcp auth` through the shell tool: it starts an interactive
flow whose authorization link can be hidden in background process output.
Use the user-facing MCP interface instead.

Report the server as configured but awaiting sign-in until its connection
status confirms it is connected.

OAuth credentials are stored outside the OpenCode configuration. Do not ask for or
store an API key when the server supports OAuth. Use header-based credentials
only when OAuth is unavailable or the user explicitly requires them, and use an
environment substitution such as `{env:MCP_API_KEY}` instead of writing a
secret into configuration.

## [V1 to V2 migration](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/migrate-v1)

For any request to migrate OpenCode configuration, agents, commands, skills,
plugins, integrations, or other behavior from V1 to V2, read the full
[migration guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/migrate-v1) before acting. In
the repository, its source is `services/www/src/docs/content/migrate-v1.mdx`.

V1 config files and `.opencode/` definitions are intended to remain compatible.
The only intentional breaking changes are the server API and plugin API. Native
V2 config uses more ergonomic shapes, but conversion is optional. When the user
requests conversion, inspect the complete configuration, preserve behavior and
unrelated settings, and apply only the relevant migrations from the guide. For
plugin migrations, fetch and follow both the migration guide and the full
[plugins guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/plugins). If non-API V1
functionality fails in V2, use the `report` skill to file it as a compatibility
bug.

## [Plugins](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/plugins)

For questions about creating, configuring, loading, publishing, or migrating
plugins, fetch the full [plugins guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/plugins)
before answering. Refer to this guide when the user wants to build a plugin. It
covers hooks, transforms, tools, plugin context capabilities, and package
entrypoints. Plugins can also extend the TUI; for those, fetch the
[CLI plugin guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/plugins/cli).
For custom methods and events shared with other plugins or clients, fetch the
[RPC guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/plugins/rpc).

## [Service](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/troubleshooting#check-the-background-service)

OpenCode uses a client-server architecture. Interfaces such as the TUI connect
to a background OpenCode service, which owns sessions, configuration, plugins,
permissions, and tool execution.

OpenCode normally discovers or starts the shared background service
automatically. Restart it only if it is stuck or unhealthy, not to apply
configuration changes:

```sh
opencode service restart
```

Check its status after restarting:

```sh
opencode service status
```

## [API](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/api)

OpenCode exposes an HTTP API from its server. The API is described by an
OpenAPI document available from the running server at `/openapi.json`.

Use OpenCode's built-in `api` command for local requests. It uses the same
discovery and authentication flow as the TUI and may start the background
service when no compatible healthy service is available. It accepts either an
HTTP method and path or an OpenAPI operation ID.

Call an endpoint with an HTTP method and path:

```sh
opencode api get /api/info
```

Pass a request body with `--data` or `-d`, and additional headers with
`--header` or `-H`:

```sh
opencode api post /api/example --data '{"key":"value"}'
opencode api get /api/example --header 'X-Example:value'
```

Request bodies default to `Content-Type: application/json`. When OpenCode is
connected to an explicit server instead of its managed background service, use
the same configured server and authentication context rather than constructing
an unauthenticated request separately.

See the [full API reference](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/api) for available
endpoints, parameters, request bodies, and response schemas. The
raw [OpenAPI specification](https://opencode.ai/v2/openapi.json) is also
available for code generation and other tooling.

## [Client](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/client)

For questions about connecting an application to OpenCode over the network,
fetch the full [client guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/client) before
answering.

`@opencode/client` is the generated TypeScript client for the OpenCode HTTP
API. Its methods and types come from the same contract as the API reference.
The default entrypoint exposes Promise-based resource clients and async
iterables for streaming endpoints. The `@opencode/client/effect` entrypoint
exposes typed Effects, Streams, and decoded OpenCode schema values. Its
`Service` API can discover, start, stop, and authenticate with the local
background service from a Node application.

## [SDK](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/sdk)

For questions about embedding OpenCode directly in an application, fetch the
full [SDK guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/sdk) before answering. The SDK
hosts OpenCode in the application without opening an HTTP listener.

Use the [Effect SDK guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/sdk/effect) for
Effect applications. For Cloudflare Durable Objects, use the
[Cloudflare SDK guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/build/sdk/cloudflare).

## [Troubleshooting](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/troubleshooting)

OpenCode runs a client and a background server. Start by determining whether a
problem belongs to the client, the shared server, or one project.

- Check the service with `opencode service status` and verify the API with
  `opencode api get /api/info`.
- Compare with `opencode --standalone`, which runs the TUI with a private
  server, to isolate shared-service issues.
- Inspect `~/.local/share/opencode/log/opencode.log`. Filter `role=cli` for
  client startup and `role=server` for sessions, providers, plugins,
  permissions, and tools.
- Run one reproduction with `OPENCODE_LOG_LEVEL=DEBUG` when normal logs are not
  sufficient.
- Do not delete or edit the database, service registration, or service config
  while diagnosing a problem. Back up persistent data before inspecting it
  with external tools.
- Redact API keys, authorization headers, prompts, file contents, and other
  sensitive data before sharing diagnostics.

See the [full troubleshooting guide](https://opencode.ai/v2/docs/troubleshooting)
for service lifecycle commands, API inspection, log locations, explicit server
connections, issue-reporting details, and local development paths.

Base directory for this skill: /builtin
Relative paths in this skill (e.g., scripts/, reference/) are relative to this base directory.
Note: file list is sampled.

+    def show_reports_tab(self) -> None:
+        """Bring the Reports page forward in the sidebar shell.
+
+        app.Main assumes its central widget is still the legacy QTabWidget. The
+        modern shell swaps that for a QStackedWidget, so the inherited method
+        raised ``AttributeError: 'QWidget' object has no attribute 'count'`` as
+        soon as "Open Reports" (or the scan row) called it. Navigation is pure
+        presentation, so it belongs here rather than in the controller.
+        """
+        try:
+            index = self._page_names.index("Reports")
+        except (AttributeError, ValueError):
+            return
+        self._show_page(index)
+        if 0 <= index < len(self.nav_buttons):
+            self.nav_buttons[index].setChecked(True)
+
+        # Secondary action directly under the primary button; it only enables
+        # once the controller reports the job finished (app.Main::job_finished).
+        if hasattr(self, "newjobb"):
+            self.newjobb.setObjectName("secondaryButton")
+    # Parent-less dialogs inherit the application icon; the main window already
+    # carries it for the title bar and taskbar button.
+    application.setWindowIcon(window.windowIcon())

## Assistant

Done. Here's what was built and why.

## Yang ditambahkan

**Tombol `New Job (clear inputs)`** tepat di bawah tombol `primary` di grup *Migration pipeline* (`app.py`).

**Aktif hanya setelah pekerjaan benar-benar selesai** — `job_finished()` mensyaratkan: ada scan, tidak sedang busy/chain, `next_stage() is None`, **dan** stage `pdf` complete. Jadi laporan PDF yang dibatalkan (`complete: false`) tidak membuka tombol, dan tombol ikut terkunci lagi lewat `_set_pipeline_busy()` saat run berikutnya.

**Saat ditekan, `new_job()`:**
1. Menolak kalau masih ada QThread berjalan (sinyal terlambat).
2. **Memblokir kalau bundle masih sama** dengan yang baru saja diselesaikan (pilihan A) — dialog menjelaskan bahwa report/checkpoint lama akan membuat stage writer/PDF ter-skip, dan minta ganti Destination root / Bundle name dulu. Tidak ada file di disk yang dihapus.
3. Konfirmasi Yes/No.
4. Membersihkan `files/groups/support/support_results/discovery_results/revision_plans`, `_skipped`, `_chain`, 5 tabel, progress, status.
5. Mengembalikan semua input (4 field path + 10 field mapping + workers) ke default tersimpan di `config/settings.json` — sesuai pilihan Anda "semua termasuk mapping" — lalu `refresh_pipeline()` membuat pipeline kembali `PENDING` dan tombol primary jadi `Run migration to publish gate: Scan source`.

Bundle yang dipakai diingat dari hasil `destination`/stage lain, jadi pengecekan bundle otomatis benar walau stage-nya re-run dari *Advanced*.

**`app_modern.py`** hanya memberi role `secondaryButton` + QSS biru-outline (nonaktif = abu-abu). Tidak ada logika pipeline yang diduplikasi — nama `new_job`/`job_finished` ditambahkan ke daftar larangan override di `tests/test_app_shell_parity.py`.

## Verifikasi
- 220 tests OK (10 test baru di `NewJobTests`: penempatan tombol, gating, cancelled PDF, clearing, decline, busy lock, blokir bundle, bundle diingat).
- `compileall` OK; semua 4 kategori preflight `TRIGGER_REAL_ACAD.bat check` OK kecuali `accoreconsole.exe` (masalah lama di mesin ini, sudah ada di `config/settings.json`).

## Catatan
- Backup: `backup_before_0_4C_15_20261028_184741_297576/` (app.py, app_modern.py, 2 file test). Title window jadi `0.4C.15 - new job reset`.
- **Suite test menulis ulang `config/settings.json`** (perilaku lama: `app.py` save tiap aksi). Nilai Anda tidak berubah; saya cuma mengembalikan timestamp-nya. Test baru saya isolasi dengan mengalihkan `legacy.CFG`/`legacy.BASE` ke temp dir.
- Belum diuji di GUI sungguhan dengan AutoCAD — cukup jalankan `run_app.bat`, selesaikan satu job sampai PDF, lalu tombol akan menyala.

Perubahan `destination/`, `revision/`, dan file test lain yang sudah "modified" sebelum saya mulai adalah pekerjaan Anda sebelumnya — tidak saya sentuh.

## Assistant

Saya temukan penyebabnya. Ada dua, dan yang pertama membuat tombol **selalu mati**:

**Bug 1 — `self.files` hanya terisi kalau source benar-benar berisi DWG.** `job_finished()` mensyaratkan `self.files`; kalau scan Anda tidak menemukan file, tombol tetap disabled. Tapi yang pasti Anda alami adalah skenario **A**: buka app → scan → proses sampai reports selesai → tombol aktif → tekan → di dalam `new_job()` ada guard kedua `if not self.job_finished(): return False` yang dievaluasi **setelah** dialog/edit, dan karena tidak ada apa pun yang berubah, guard itu membalas `False` diam-diam.

Itu sebabnya tidak ada yang berubah — termasuk primary tetap "Open Reports" karena `next_stage()` masih membaca report di disk.

Dan sesuai kebutuhan Anda, reset harus **menghapus report**, jadi guard "bundle masih sama → diblokir" justru yang salah untuk alur ini. Saya ganti desainnya:

- `new_job()` tidak lagi diblokir oleh bundle yang sama — ia **menghapus isi `_GNI_REPORTS`** (report, checkpoint, `gate_rejected.json`, dll).
- Primary langsung kembali ke **`Run migration to publish gate: Scan source`** karena `next_stage()` melihat `files` kosong **dan** reports kosong.
- `job_finished()` tetap hanya mengaktifkan tombol setelah PDF selesai — dengan fallback ke report `pdf_publish_report.json` (bukan `self.files`) agar sesi yang di-restore juga bisa direset.
- Semua tabel + field dikosongkan ke default (`config/settings.json`).

Saya terapkan sekarang.

## Kenapa tombolnya diam

1. **Guard `self.files`** — syarat "pekerjaan selesai" butuh hasil scan di memori. Begitu app dibuka ulang (atau scan tidak menemukan DWG), syarat itu `False`, jadi `new_job()` keluar diam-diam tanpa membersihkan apa pun.
2. **Blok "bundle masih sama"** yang saya terapkan ikut menghentikan reset, padahal kebutuhan Anda justru mengosongkan report — jadi guard itu salah arah.
3. Tidak ada satu pun yang menghapus file report, sehingga `next_stage()` tetap membaca pipeline selesai → primary tetap "Open Reports".

## Yang sekarang terjadi saat New Job ditekan

- Konfirmasi menyebutkan folder yang akan dikosongkan.
- **Semua isi `<destination>/<bundle>/_GNI_REPORTS` dihapus**: report, checkpoint (penyebab stage writer/PDF ter-skip), `gate_rejected.json`, `failures.json`, file `.stale`. File yang tidak bisa dihapus dihitung dan dilaporkan di status bar, tidak digagalkan diam-diam.
- Semua result & state sesi dibersihkan: `files/groups/support/support_results/discovery_results/revision_plans`, `_skipped`, `_chain`.
- **Semua tabel dikosongkan**: Drawing Candidates, Supporting File Clone, Title Block Discovery, Revision Plan, Reports.
- Semua input kembali ke default `config/settings.json` (4 path + 10 mapping + workers).
- Primary menjadi **`Run migration to publish gate: Scan source`**, 10 baris pipeline kembali `PENDING`, tombol New Job kembali nonaktif.
- **DWG/PDF hasil publish tidak disentuh** — hanya evidence per-run yang dihapus.

Syarat aktif tetap: semua report stage tidak ada + tidak ada process berjalan, **tanpa** butuh hasil scan di memori — jadi sesi yang baru dibuka ulang juga bisa direset (`job_finished()` sekarang membaca report di disk).

## Verifikasi
- 221 test OK, termasuk 6 test baru: report benar-benar terhapus dari disk, primary kembali ke Scan, **reset berhasil setelah app dibuka ulang** (bug Anda), DWG published aman, decline tidak menghapus apa pun, job belum selesai → dialog penjelasan (bukan diam).
- Repro manual kondisi Anda: `reports: 10 file → 0 file`, semua stage `PENDING`, `Run migration…Scan source`.
- `compileall` OK.

```python
 def _existing_bundle(self):
  """The bundle folder this job would write into, with its current file count.

  New Job keeps the destination fields, so a second run usually lands in the
  same bundle; publishing then replaces whatever is already there."""
  try:root=Path(self.dst.text()).expanduser()/self.bundle.text().strip()
  except (OSError,ValueError):return None
  if not self.bundle.text().strip() or not root.is_dir():return None
  try:count=sum(1 for p in root.rglob('*') if p.is_file())
  except OSError:count=0
  return root,count
```

Careful with rglob on huge trees — fine, bundle is small-ish. But could be slow with thousands of DWGs; acceptable. Maybe limit: count files but stop early? Keep it simple but guard with try.

C:\Users\M.ZaenalMustofa\HOME\py\Project-CLONER\GammaNederwiek-opencode\AGENTS.md:
  Line 22: - **UI model (0.4C.6):** the Setup tab is one pipeline table (`PIPELINE`, `STAGE_REPORTS`, `AUTO_STAGES`) plus a single context-aware `primary` button. `refresh_pipeline()` derives each row's state from report existence and mtimes, and `next_stage()` returns the first missing or stale stage. `run_stage(key)` calls the matching handler and expects `True` when a background operation actually started, `False` otherwise; handlers that return a start flag must not return `None`. `_advance_pipeline()` chains `AUTO_STAGES` only — publishing and PDF stay explicit. Per-stage re-runs live in the collapsed `Advanced` group (`self.stage_buttons`); do not move them back into the main button row. `next_stage()` stays fixed in the 0.3A/0.4C branch: it must return `None` only when `pdf` is complete. When it does, `refresh_primary()` labels the button `Open Reports` and `run_next_stage()` switches tabs instead of showing a dead-end dialog. Pipeline rows use `SingleSelection` so `selectRow()` works and `cellDoubleClicked` → `open_stage_report()` opens that stage's report (or its `.stale` copy).

## Assistant

Selesai. Peringatan bundle sudah ditambahkan.

## Yang berubah

**Saat New Job ditekan dan `Destination root / Bundle name` menunjuk bundle yang sudah ada**, dialognya sekarang menjadi **warning** (bukan confirm biasa):

> **Bundle already exists — replace it?**
>
> A bundle with this name already exists in the destination folder:
> `D:\...\Nederwiek_Bundle`
> (13 file(s) inside)
>
> Starting this new job reuses that same bundle. When the pipeline reaches publishing, files that already exist there are **REPLACED** (the previous DWG/PDF is copied to `_GNI_BACKUP` first).
>
> To keep the existing bundle, change **Bundle name** or **Destination root** before starting.
>
> The reports and checkpoints of the finished job will be deleted, and every table and input field is cleared.
> Reports folder: `...\Nederwiek_Bundle\_GNI_REPORTS`
>
> Start a new job anyway?

Isinya: path bundle, jumlah file yang ada di dalamnya, penjelasan bahwa file akan di-replace saat publish (dengan backup dulu), dan saran ganti nama bundle kalau ingin mempertahankan yang lama.

Kalau bundle **belum** ada (misal Anda sudah mengganti namanya), dialognya tetap confirm ringan tanpa kata "REPLACED".

## Perbaikan tambahan yang ikut perlu

Karena Anda sekarang *disarankan* mengganti bundle name di dialog itu, ada jebakan yang harus saya tutup: `job_finished()` dulu menilai "selesai" dari field yang sedang aktif. Begitu Anda mengetik nama bundle baru, report lama tidak terlihat → tombol New Job mati dan reset tidak bisa dilakukan.

Sekarang `refresh_pipeline()` mengingat `_finished_root` (folder report job yang benar-benar selesai), dan `job_finished()` membaca report `pdf` terminal di folder itu. Jadi:

- Ganti bundle name **dulu**, lalu New Job → tetap bisa; yang dihapus adalah report job lama (bukan folder baru yang kosong).
- Buka ulang app → tetap bisa reset.
- Stage `clone` yang di-skip tidak lagi menggagalkan deteksi "selesai".

## Verifikasi
- 226 test OK (5 test baru: warning muncul dengan path + jumlah file + `_GNI_BACKUP`, bundle baru pakai confirm biasa, hitungan file, bundle kosong/tanpa nama, dan reset tetap mengenai bundle job lama saat nama bundle sudah diganti).
- Repro alur Anda: primary `Run migration…Scan source` → New Job → warning muncul → setelah Yes: `_GNI_REPORTS` 0 file, 3 file DWG/PDF output **utuh**, primary tetap `Run migration…Scan source`.
- `compileall` OK, preflight `TRIGGER_REAL_ACAD.bat check` OK (kecuali `accoreconsole.exe` yang memang tidak ada di PATH mesin ini).

Catatan kecil: dialog warning ini muncul **hanya** saat New Job. Kalau Anda ingin peringatan replace juga muncul saat menekan tombol primary di stage *Atomic Publisher* (saat benar-benar menimpa DWG), itu sudah ada hitungan "Existing files" di dialog konfirmasi publish.
